```
Similarly the context update `update Gamma x T`. They have the same name structure but different types. Fine.

Let's write the helper:
```
Lemma env_ok_update : forall Gamma sigma x T u,
  env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T u ->
  env_ok (update Gamma x T) (env_update sigma x u).
Proof.
  intros Gamma sigma x T u Henv Hu y S Hy.

  - apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy. injection Hy as <-. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. exact Hu.
    (* careful: Hy : (if eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y) = Some S; after subst y, eqb x x; and the goal's env_update x = if eqb x x then u else... *)
  - apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. rewrite E in Hy. apply Henv. exact Hy.
Qed.
```

  | nil => 0

Proof.

  - contradiction.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + apply Nat.le_trans with (max_list l); [apply IH; exact Hx | apply Nat.le_max_r].

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary lemmas *)

Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => x :: nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2

  | tm_false => nil
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Lemma in_max_list : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hx; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hx.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eauto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall l1 l2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x l1 /\ ~ In x l2.
Proof.
  intros l1 l2. exists (S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))). split; intro H.
  - apply in_max_list in H.
    assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))).
    { apply Nat.lt_succ_r. exact H. }
    apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
  - apply in_max_list in H.
    assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))).
    { apply Nat.lt_succ_r. exact H. }
    apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
Qed.
```

```
Lemma lc_open_rec : forall (t : tm) (k : nat) (u : tm),
  lc_at (S k) t -> lc_at k u -> lc_at k (open_rec k u t).
Proof.
  induction t; intros k u Ht Hu; simpl.
  - (* bvar i *) destruct (Nat.eqb k i) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst i; exact Hu.
    + apply lc_bvar. inversion Ht; subst. apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. 
      (* i < S k and i <> k → i < k *)

For the main relation R:

```coq
Fixpoint R (T : ty) (t : tm) {struct T} : Prop :=
  locally_closed t /\
  strongly_normalizing t /\
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => True
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => forall u, R T1 u -> R T2 (tm_app t u)
  end.
```

Note: Coq requires the Fixpoint's recursive calls on structurally smaller arguments. T1 and T2 are subterms of Ty_Arrow T1 T2 ✓. The definition is accepted.

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary definitions and lemmas for the normalization proof. *)

(** Free variables of a term. *)
Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => x :: nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3
  end.

(** Maximum of a list of naturals, used to pick fresh atoms. *)
Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Lemma in_max_list : forall (l : list nat) (x : nat), In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hx; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hx.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eauto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall l1 l2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x l1 /\ ~ In x l2.
Proof.
  intros l1 l2. exists (S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))). split; intro H;
  apply in_max_list in H;
  assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)))
    by (apply Nat.lt_succ_r; exact H);
  apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
Qed.

(** ** Monotonicity and opening lemmas for local closure. *)

Lemma lc_at_mono : forall (t : tm) (k k' : nat), k <= k' -> lc_at k t -> lc_at k' t.

    intros k k' Hle Hlc; inversion Hlc; subst; simpl.

  - apply lc_fvar.
  - apply lc_app; [apply IH1 with (k := k) | apply IH2 with (k := k)]; assumption.
  - apply lc_abs. apply IH1 with (k := S k). lia. assumption.
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
  - apply lc_if; [apply IH1 with (k := k) | apply IH2 with (k := k) | apply IH3 with (k := k)]; assumption.
Qed.

And `env_ok_update`:
```
Lemma env_ok_update : forall Gamma sigma x T u,
  env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T u ->
  env_ok (update Gamma x T) (env_update sigma x u).
Proof.
  intros Gamma sigma x T u [Hlcs Henv] Hu. split.
  - intros y. unfold env_update. destruct (Nat.eqb x y); [apply R_lc in Hu; exact Hu | apply Hlcs].
  - intros y S Hy. unfold update, env_update in *.
    destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst y. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy. injection Hy as <-. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. exact Hu.
    + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. rewrite E in Hy. apply Henv. exact Hy.
Qed.
```

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary development for strong normalization. *)

(** Free variables of a term. *)
Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => x :: nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Lemma in_max_list : forall (l : list nat) (x : nat), In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hx; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hx.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eauto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall l1 l2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x l1 /\ ~ In x l2.
Proof.
  intros l1 l2. exists (S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))). split; intro H;
    apply in_max_list in H;
    assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)))
      by (apply Nat.lt_succ_r; exact H);
    apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
Qed.
```

One more consideration: in the fundamental theorem, the induction is on Hty : has_type Gamma t T, with the environment sigma. The goal after `induction Hty` is `forall sigma, env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T (subst_env sigma t)`. I need to write the tactic properly:
```
Theorem fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma, env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T (subst_env sigma t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T Hty.
  induction Hty as [Gamma x T Heq
                 | L Gamma T1 T2 t1 Hpre IHpre
                 | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
                 | Gamma
                 | Gamma
                 | Gamma t1 t2 t3 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2 H3 IH3];
  intros sigma Henv.
  ...
```
Wait, after `intros Gamma t T Hty`, the goal is `forall sigma, env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T (subst_env sigma t)`, and Gamma, t, T are generalized by the induction? Hmm, Hty : has_type Gamma t T with Gamma, t, T variables; the goal mentions them. `induction Hty` — the induction principle's motive P Gamma t T := forall sigma, env_ok Gamma sigma -> R T (subst_env sigma t). Coq will generalize the goal appropriately ✓. The intro pattern per constructor:
- T_Var: forall Gamma x T, Gamma x = Some T -> P Gamma (fvar x) T. Pattern: Gamma x T Heq (no IH).
- T_Abs: forall L Gamma T1 T2 t1, (forall x, ~In x L -> has_type ...) -> (forall x, ~In x L -> P ...) -> P Gamma (abs T1 t1) (T1->T2). Pattern: L Gamma T1 T2 t1 Hpre IHpre.
- T_App: Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2.
- T_True: Gamma.
- T_False: Gamma.
- T_If: Gamma t1 t2 t3 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2 H3 IH3.

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary development for the strong normalization proof. *)

(** Free variables of a term, used to pick fresh atoms. *)

Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => x :: nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Lemma in_max_list : forall (l : list nat) (x : nat), In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hx; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hx.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eauto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall l1 l2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x l1 /\ ~ In x l2.
Proof.
  intros l1 l2. exists (S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))). split; intro H;
    apply in_max_list in H;
    assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)))
      by (apply Nat.lt_succ_r; exact H);
    apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
Qed.
```

Wait, in the assert: the goal is `S m < S m` where m = max_list (l1++l2); `apply Nat.lt_succ_r` turns it into `S m <= m`. And H : S m <= max_list (l1++l2) = S m? H is `S (max_list (l1++l2)) <= max_list (l1++l2)`, so exactly H ✓.

Lemma lc_at_mono : forall (t : tm) (k k' : nat),
  k <= k' -> lc_at k t -> lc_at k' t.

  - apply lc_fvar.
  - apply lc_app; [apply (IH1 k k' Hle) | apply (IH2 k k' Hle)]; assumption.
  - apply lc_abs. apply (IH1 (S k) (S k')); [lia | assumption].
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
  - apply lc_if; [apply (IH1 k k' Hle) | apply (IH2 k k' Hle) | apply (IH3 k k' Hle)]; assumption.
Qed.
```

But the branch tactics themselves leave the lc_at k t1 goal; so after both branches, we have two goals, both solved by assumption ✓. Actually the `;` binds to the entire `apply lc_app; [...]`? Precedence: `t1; t2` applies t2 to all goals generated by t1. So `apply lc_app; [tac1 | tac2]; assumption` = apply lc_app, then apply the brackets to each goal, then assumption to all remaining. ✓

Lemma lc_open_rec : forall (t : tm) (k : nat) (u : tm),
  lc_at (S k) t -> lc_at k u -> lc_at k (open_rec k u t).
Proof.
  intros t. induction t as [i | x | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 | T0 t1 IH1 | | | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 t3 IH3];

  - apply lc_false.

Qed.

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary development for the strong normalization proof. *)

(** Free variables of a term, used to pick fresh atoms. *)
Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => x :: nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Lemma in_max_list : forall (l : list nat) (x : nat), In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hx; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hx.
  - destruct Hx as [-> | Hx].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eauto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall l1 l2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x l1 /\ ~ In x l2.
Proof.
  intros l1 l2. exists (S (max_list (l1 ++ l2))). split; intro H;
    apply in_max_list in H;
    assert (Hlt : S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)) < S (max_list (l1 ++ l2)))
      by (apply Nat.lt_succ_r; exact H);
    apply (Nat.lt_irrefl _ Hlt).
Qed.

Now the final theorem:
```
Theorem normalization : forall t T,
  <{ empty |-- t \in T }> ->
  strongly_normalizing t.
Proof.
  intros t T Hty.
  pose proof (fundamental empty t T Hty (fun x => tm_fvar x)) as HR.
  assert (Henv : env_ok empty (fun x => tm_fvar x)).
  { split.
    - intros x. apply lc_fvar.
    - intros x S Hx. unfold empty in Hx. discriminate. }
  specialize (HR Henv).
  rewrite subst_env_id in HR.

Now subst_env and subst_env_id:

```
Fixpoint subst_env (sigma : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
  | tm_fvar x => sigma x
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (subst_env sigma t1) (subst_env sigma t2)
  | tm_abs T t1 => tm_abs T (subst_env sigma t1)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => tm_if (subst_env sigma t1) (subst_env sigma t2) (subst_env sigma t3)
  end.

Proof.
  induction t; simpl; congruence.
Qed.
```

Lemma subst_env_open_rec : forall (t : tm) (sigma : atom -> tm) (x : atom) (u : tm) (k : nat),
  (forall y, locally_closed (sigma y)) ->
  ~ In x (fv t) ->
  subst_env (env_update sigma x u) (open_rec k (tm_fvar x) t)
    = open_rec k u (subst_env sigma t).
Proof.
  intros t. induction t as [i | y | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 | T0 t1 IH1 | | | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 t3 IH3];
    intros sigma x u k Hsig Hx; simpl.

    + unfold env_update. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity.
    + reflexivity.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. exfalso. apply Hx. simpl. left. symmetry. exact E.
    + unfold env_update. rewrite E. 
      (* goal: sigma y = open_rec k u (sigma y) *)
      symmetry. apply lc_open_rec_id with (k := 0); [apply Hsig | lia].
  - (* app *) rewrite IH1, IH2; [reflexivity | exact Hsig | exact Hx | exact Hsig | exact Hx].
  - (* abs *) rewrite IH1; [reflexivity | exact Hsig | exact Hx].
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - (* if *) rewrite IH1, IH2, IH3; try reflexivity; try exact Hsig; try exact Hx.

Let me handle freshness with a helper lemma: `~ In x (a ++ b) -> ~ In x a` and `~ In x (a ++ b) -> ~ In x b`. I can just do:
```

For the if case: `fv (tm_if t1 t2 t3) = fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3` which parses as `(fv t1 ++ fv t2) ++ fv t3`. So Hx : ~ In x ((fv t1 ++ fv t2) ++ fv t3). Hx1: ~ In x (fv t1) via in_or_app left then in_or_app left. Hx2: left then right. Hx3: right. I'll write helper lemmas:

```
Lemma not_in_app_l : forall (x : atom) (l1 l2 : list atom), ~ In x (l1 ++ l2) -> ~ In x l1.
Proof. intros x l1 l2 H Hin. apply H. apply in_or_app. left. exact Hin. Qed.
Lemma not_in_app_r : forall (x : atom) (l1 l2 : list atom), ~ In x (l1 ++ l2) -> ~ In x l2.
Proof. intros x l1 l2 H Hin. apply H. apply in_or_app. right. exact Hin. Qed.
```
For the if case, fv (tm_if t1 t2 t3) = fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3. Parsing: `++` is right associative? In Coq, `++` (app) is right associative at level 60? Let me check: `Infix "++" := app (right associativity, at level 60)`. Yes, right associative. So `fv t1 ++ fv t2 ++ fv t3` = `fv t1 ++ (fv t2 ++ fv t3)`. Hmm!
